- **token-discipline**: Session-start operating defaults that cut token spend without cutting quality, tuned for Claude Opus 5. Targets where the tokens actually are — 96% of a session's bill is reused input, resent from the top every turn — so it addresses repetition, delegation and read width rather than prose length. Ships as a ~1029-byte literal designed to sit inside the cached system prefix, paid for once per cache window rather than re-read. Includes the reasoning for every rule, the four alternatives that were rejected as traps (tool-definition stripping, transport-layer prefix truncation, request-hash respo… ([DiologIR/diolog-plugins](https://github.com/DiologIR/diolog-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/token-discipline))

- **token-economy**: Cut INPUT/orchestration tokens (and add output discipline) for multi-agent work.
